<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding:0pt; text-indent:0pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000"><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding:0pt; text-indent:0pt; font-size:10pt; color:#000000"><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:36pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:36pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=center><U>PART III</U></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:36pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:36pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:-12pt; padding-left:0pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:36pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>Item 10.</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:0pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:36pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:72pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><U>DIRECTORS AND EXECUTIVE OFFICERS OF THE REGISTRANT</U></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:36pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:28.8pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>The information required with respect to directors and the audit committee of Bairnco is included in the Proxy Statement for the 2006 Annual Meeting of Stockholders of Bairnco, to be filed with the Securities and Exchange Commission no later than 120 days after December 31, 2005, and is incorporated herein by reference. </P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:28.8pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:28.8pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>See the information regarding executive officers of the Corporation on page 13 of this Annual Report on Form 10-K.</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:28.8pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:28.8pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>On January 29, 2004, the Board of Directors of Bairnco Corporation (the &#147;Board&#148;) adopted the <I>Financial Code of Ethical Conduct</I>, which applies to the Corporation&#146;s Chief Executive Officer and the chief financial officer, the other financial officers and other financial managers. &nbsp;The <I>Financial Code of Ethical</I> Conduct is posted on the Corporation&#146;s internet site at www.bairnco.com.</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:28.8pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:28.8pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>The Board has determined that the Corporation has at least one audit committee financial expert serving on its audit committee. &nbsp;Gerald L. DeGood, Chairman of the Audit Committee, qualifies as an audit committee financial expert and is an independent member of the Board. &nbsp;Mr. DeGood meets the independence requirements as set forth in the Exchange Act Section 10A(m)(3).</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:-12pt; padding-left:0pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>Item 11.</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:0pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:72pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><U>EXECUTIVE COMPENSATION</U></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:28.8pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>The information required by Item 11 is included in the Proxy Statement for the 2006 Annual Meeting of Stockholders of Bairnco, to be filed with the Securities and Exchange Commission no later than 120 days after December 31, 2005, and is incorporated herein by reference.</P>

<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:0pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:27pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:-12pt; padding-left:72pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:0pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-36pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>(1)</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:72pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:0pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>Audit fees consisted of audit work (including the tax accrual review) performed in connection with the preparation of the quarterly and year end financial statements, European statutory audits and review of documents filed with the SEC. </P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:-36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:0pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:36pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:-12pt; padding-left:72pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:0pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-36pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>(2)</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:72pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:0pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>Audit related fees consisted of audits of employee benefit plans, consulting related to Section 404 of the Sarbanes-Oxley Act (Ernst &amp; Young LLP only) and a review of the NOL Lawsuit settlement (Grant Thornton LLP only).</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:-36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:0pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:36pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:-12pt; padding:0pt; text-indent:36pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>(3)</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding:0pt; text-indent:72pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>Tax fees consisted of assistance related to tax compliance and reporting.</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:-36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:0pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:36pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:0pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:27pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><I>Pre-Approval of Audit &amp; Non-Audit Services</I> &#150; Under the Corporation&#146;s Audit Committee Charter, the Audit Committee of the Board of Directors (&#147;Audit Committee&#148;) is required to pre-approve all auditing services and permissible non-audit services, including related fees and terms, to be performed for the Corporation by the independent auditor, subject to de-minimus exceptions for non-audit services described under Section 10A(i)(1)(B) of the Securities Exchange Act of 1934 which are approved by the Audit Committee prior to the completion of the engagement, provided that such services had not been recognized as non-audit services at the time of the engagement. &nbsp;</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:0pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:27pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:-0.95pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:27pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>All pre-approvals must take place in advance of the services within a period not to exceed three months prior to the start of the engagement. The proposals submitted by the independent accountant to the Audit Committee for pre-approval must be specific as to the type of service and provide detail of the scope of work and fee estimate, including out-of-pocket expenses. The proposals must include detailed back-up documentation regarding the specific services to be provided. Any expected change in the audit scope or related fees from the pre-approved amounts will also require pre-approval in advance of the additional work.</P>
<P style="margin:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:-0.95pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:27pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:-0.95pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:27pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>The Committee may form and delegate authority to subcommittees consisting of one or more of its members when appropriate, including the authority to grant pre-approvals of audit and permissible non-audit services, provided that decisions of such subcommittees to grant pre-approvals shall be presented to the full Committee at its next scheduled meeting. The Committee will not delegate to management any of the Committee&#146;s responsibilities related to pre-approval of audit and permissible non-audit services.</P>
<P style="margin:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:-0.95pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:27pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:-0.95pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:27pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>The Audit Committee pre-approved all audit services, audit related services and tax review and compliance services for the Corporation by Grant Thornton LLP during 2005.</P>
<P style="margin:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:-0.95pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding:0pt; text-indent:0pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000"><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding:0pt; text-indent:0pt; line-height:10pt; font-size:10pt; color:#000000" align=center>#</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding:0pt; text-indent:0pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000"><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding:0pt; text-indent:0pt; font-size:10pt; color:#000000"><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:36pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=center><U>PART IV</U></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=center><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-right:0pt; margin-bottom:-12pt; padding-left:72pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:-0.95pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-72pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>Item 15.</P>
<P style="margin:0pt; padding-left:72pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:-0.95pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><U>EXHIBITS, FINANCIAL STATEMENT SCHEDULES AND REPORTS ON FORM 8-K</U></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:-12pt; padding-left:18pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:13.5pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>a)</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:18pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:54pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>1. &nbsp;<U>Financial Statements</U></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:93.6pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>Included in the 2005 Annual Report to Stockholders which is included as Exhibit 13 to this Annual Report on Form 10-K:</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:36pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:57.6pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify><BR></P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:-12pt; padding-left:122.4pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-28.8pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>&#183;</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:122.4pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>Reports of Independent Registered Public Accounting Firms;</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:-12pt; padding-left:121.5pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:-27.9pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>&#183;</P>
<P style="margin-left:0pt; margin-top:0pt; margin-bottom:0pt; padding-left:121.5pt; padding-top:0pt; padding-right:3.6pt; padding-bottom:0pt; text-indent:0pt; line-height:12pt; font-size:12pt; color:#000000" align=justify>Consolidated Statements of Operations for the years ended December 31, 2005, 2004 and 2003;</P>
